# ZirconEngine Bevy 完成度两层路线图

## Summary

以本地 `dev/bevy` 源码为主基准：`bevy 0.19.0-dev`，commit `c040d7603`；docs.rs `latest` 当前为 `bevy 0.18.1`，仅作为公开 API 文档引用。路线分两层推进：

1. **基础设施层**：补齐 Bevy 风格的 `PluginGroup`、`DefaultPlugins`/`MinimalPlugins`、稳定 prelude、State、Time、任务池、log、diagnostics、dev profile。
2. **完整体验层**：把 Zircon 的默认启动体验扩展到接近 Bevy `DefaultPlugins` 的组合完成度，包括窗口/输入、资产、场景、渲染、UI、热更新、开发诊断。

参考依据包括 Bevy `DefaultPlugins`/`MinimalPlugins` 源码 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:5)、Bevy prelude [prelude.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/prelude.rs:5)、公开文档 [DefaultPlugins](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/struct.DefaultPlugins.html)、[PluginGroup](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/app/trait.PluginGroup.html)、[Time](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/time/struct.Time.html)、[States](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/state/state/trait.States.html)、[LogPlugin](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/log/struct.LogPlugin.html)、[DiagnosticsPlugin](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/diagnostic/struct.DiagnosticsPlugin.html)。

## Key Changes

- 在 `zircon_app` 增加 `plugins` 组合层：`PluginGroup`、`PluginGroupBuilder`、`DefaultPlugins`、`MinimalPlugins`、`DevPlugins`、`HeadlessPlugins`。该层只负责编排现有 `ModuleDescriptor`/runtime modules，不替代 `CoreRuntime` 生命周期。
- `PluginGroupBuilder` 支持 `set`、`disable`、`enable`、`add_before`、`add_after`、`finish`，语义对齐 Bevy `PluginGroupBuilder`，并对重复 key、缺失锚点、禁用后排序等场景做显式错误。
- 在 `zircon_runtime::core` 下补齐 `state`、`time`、`tasks`、`diagnostics` 公共模块；保留现有 `CoreRuntime`、service registry、module lifecycle，只增加类型化访问器和默认基础服务。
- 增加稳定 prelude：`zircon_runtime::prelude::*`、`zircon_app::prelude::*`，导出 `CoreRuntime`、`CoreHandle`、module 描述符、默认插件组、State/Time/Task/Diagnostics/Log 常用类型。
- 日志层保持现有 `diagnostic_log` 兼容，同时增加 Bevy 式 `LogSettings`：`filter`、`level`、console/file sink、`ZIRCON_LOG` 过滤串；`ZIRCON_LOG_LEVEL` 继续兼容，`RUST_LOG` 在 `ZIRCON_LOG` 缺省时可用。

## Current Implementation Status - 2026-05-20

当前基础设施层 M1-M6 已进入“源码与文档完成、广域 Cargo 验收待统一测试窗口”的状态。M7 的 platform/window/input 基础也已推进到 Bevy 式能力声明、窗口 DTO、monitor/video-mode 选择和 app 诊断可见阶段；验收尚未标记为最终完成，是因为当前共享 checkout 内仍有多条活跃会话和 Cargo/rustc 进程，且 asset/render/UI/material/sound 等 owner lane 仍会影响全仓编译结论。

| Milestone | Status | Zircon 落点 | Bevy 依据 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M1 App 组合层 | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `zircon_app::plugins` 已提供 `PluginGroup`、`PluginGroupBuilder`、`DefaultPlugins`、`MinimalPlugins`、`DevPlugins`、`HeadlessPlugins`，runner diagnostics 可在 bootstrap 前解释模块选择。 | Bevy `PluginGroup`/builder 排序与禁用语义见 [plugin_group.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_app/src/plugin_group.rs:174)，默认组合见 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:5)，公开 API 见 [PluginGroup docs](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/app/trait.PluginGroup.html)。 |
| M2 Stable Prelude | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `zircon_runtime::prelude` 与 `zircon_app::prelude` 已导出 runtime/app 基础设施、profile 诊断、Time、TaskPools、log diagnostics、`LogSettings`、platform/input 稳定子集。 | Bevy prelude 的核心类型重导出形态见 [prelude.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/prelude.rs:2)。 |
| M3 State | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `zircon_runtime::core::state` 已提供 runtime-wide state contracts 与 transition hooks，保持不引入 Bevy ECS 的有意差异。 | Bevy state trait 与 transition schedule 形态见 [states.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_state/src/state/states.rs:64)、[transitions.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_state/src/state/transitions.rs:19)，公开 API 见 [States docs](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/state/state/trait.States.html)。 |
| M4 Time | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `RuntimeTimeClocks` / `RuntimeTimeAdvance` 推进 real、virtual、fixed clocks，并把 frame count、fixed steps、frame time、FPS 记录到 runtime-owned diagnostics store。 | Bevy `TimePlugin` 与 real/virtual/fixed 资源形态见 [bevy_time/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_time/src/lib.rs:59)，公开 API 见 [Time docs](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/time/struct.Time.html)。 |
| M5 Tasks | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `zircon_runtime::core::tasks` 已提供 IO、async-compute、compute `TaskPools`、线程分配配置、runtime/handle 报告和稳定诊断行输出。 | Bevy `TaskPoolPlugin`、`TaskPoolOptions`、IO/async-compute/compute 分配策略见 [task_pool_plugin.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_app/src/task_pool_plugin.rs:24)。 |
| M6 Log / Diagnostics / Dev Profile | 已落地，等待最终全仓验证 | `DiagnosticStore`、Time diagnostics、diagnostic-store snapshot logging、dev-profile cadence、`ZIRCON_LOG_FILTER`/`ZIRCON_LOG`/`RUST_LOG` 过滤优先级、`DiagnosticLogSettings`/`LogSettings`、console/file sink toggles 已落地。 | Bevy `LogPlugin` 的默认 filter、`RUST_LOG` 覆盖和全局 subscriber 约束见 [bevy_log/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_log/src/lib.rs:69)、[bevy_log/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_log/src/lib.rs:289)，诊断输出 cadence 见 [log_diagnostics_plugin.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_diagnostic/src/log_diagnostics_plugin.rs:23)。 |

M7-M12 仍是完整体验层与开发工具层：窗口/输入、资产热更新、场景/渲染/UI、脚本/editor-host、profiling/tracing、overlay 与系统信息诊断都应继续按独立里程碑推进，并在每条线进入测试阶段时合并到全仓验证。

### Plugin Ecosystem Continuation - 2026-05-31

Rendering optional feature editor-module capability parity is now a completed
plugin-ecosystem slice. The eight Rendering optional features keep their owner
model under `rendering`, but their editor modules now also project
`editor.feature.rendering.<feature>` in the generated provider manifest, static
`zircon_plugins/rendering/plugin.toml`, built-in catalog descriptor, and feature
editor crate descriptors. Focused validation passed for the Rendering provider
manifest test, built-in catalog regression, static `plugin.toml` round-trip
parity, and all eight feature editor crate checks. This improves editor-host and
Hub package projection fidelity; it does not close broad M10 render promotion,
which still requires a refreshed render owner and staged render validation.

Rendering umbrella static metadata parity is now also completed. Static
`zircon_plugins/rendering/plugin.toml` explicitly publishes `sdk_api_version`,
category `rendering`, stable maturity, client/editor `supported_targets`, and
the package-level `runtime.plugin.rendering` capability, matching the built-in
catalog and descriptor-derived package manifest used by availability,
optional-feature dependency checks, and export planning. Focused red/green
validation covered the static TOML/catalog regression. This is metadata parity
only and does not promote Rendering feature runtime behavior or broad M10 render
acceptance.

Sound static metadata parity is now completed for the runtime/editor audio
package. Static `zircon_plugins/sound/plugin.toml` explicitly publishes
`sdk_api_version`, category `runtime`, beta maturity, client/editor
`supported_targets`, and the package-level `runtime.plugin.sound` capability,
matching the built-in catalog and descriptor-derived package manifest before
optional timeline and ray-traced reverb feature rows. Focused red/green
validation covered the static TOML/catalog regression. This is metadata parity
only and does not promote Sound mixer, output backend, spatial audio, HRTF, or
dynamic-events runtime acceptance.

Net content-download dependency parity is also completed for this continuation.
`net.content_download` now declares its required `runtime.feature.net.http`
dependency consistently in the linked Net package manifest, static
`zircon_plugins/net/plugin.toml`, and the already-correct content-download
provider crate. Focused red/green validation passed for the base Net package
manifest, static plugin TOML round-trip, and the content-download provider lib
tests. This improves Plugin Manager/export dependency gating; it does not claim
production CDN/cache completion.

Net built-in catalog parity is now completed as the next plugin-ecosystem slice.
`RuntimePluginDescriptor::builtin_catalog()` exposes the same six Net owner
optional feature rows as static `zircon_plugins/net/plugin.toml` and linked
`zircon_plugin_net_runtime::package_manifest()`: HTTP, WebSocket, RPC,
replication, reliable UDP, and content download. The built-in dependency report
also blocks Content Download when `runtime.feature.net.http` is unavailable.
Focused validation passed for the built-in catalog row set, content-download
dependency report, static plugin TOML dependency, and linked Net package
manifest.

Net linked runtime metadata parity is now completed as a follow-up. Static
`zircon_plugins/net/plugin.toml`, linked
`zircon_plugin_net_runtime::package_manifest()`, and the built-in catalog all
classify Net as category `runtime`, maturity `beta`, and
`runtime.plugin.net` status `partial` with the Bevy Remote reference. Focused
red/green validation passed for both linked package metadata and static TOML
metadata.

Net static target metadata parity is now also completed. Static
`zircon_plugins/net/plugin.toml` explicitly publishes `sdk_api_version`,
server/client/editor `supported_targets`, and package-level
`runtime.plugin.net` capability, matching the built-in catalog and linked
runtime descriptor target shape used for availability, optional feature
dependency checks, and export planning. The `net.runtime` module target row now
also includes `editor_host`, so package availability covers the package's
runtime/editor module rows. Focused validation covered the static TOML/catalog
regression and linked Net runtime package metadata. This does not promote
production networking, transport backends, replication, RPC, or content
download behavior.

First-party runtime event catalog metadata is now no longer allowed to be an
empty placeholder. Static and linked package manifests for Net, Particles, and
Sound publish concrete event rows under `net.runtime_events`,
`particles.dynamic_events`, and `sound.dynamic_events`; the static manifest
contract now rejects declared event catalogs whose `events` array is empty.
2026-05-31 focused validation passed the event-catalog row contract (1),
the full static `plugin_tomls_declare` contract set (38), and linked
Net/Particles/Sound manifest filters (1/1/3) with `--locked`, `--offline`,
`--jobs 1`, and target dir `D:\cargo-targets\zircon-plugin-event-catalogs`.
This improves plugin-window, runtime-report, and export metadata fidelity only;
it does not promote Net transport behavior, Particles simulation/VFX behavior,
or Sound dynamic-event execution beyond their existing scoped evidence.

Bevy source traceability for plugin capability statuses is now machine-checked.
Static capability-status `bevy_references` entries for Animation, Net, and
Sound must stay as repository-relative paths under `dev/bevy` and resolve to
existing files before readiness, plugin-window, or export metadata accepts the
status rows. 2026-05-31 focused validation passed the Bevy-reference contract
(1) and the full static `plugin_tomls_declare` contract set (39) with
`--locked`, `--offline`, `--jobs 1`, and target dir
`D:\cargo-targets\zircon-plugin-bevy-refs`.

Static package/API version semantics are now machine-checked across the plugin
ecosystem. Every static `zircon_plugins/**/plugin.toml` top-level `version` and
`sdk_api_version` value must be a strict numeric `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH` core with
no leading zeroes before plugin-window, runtime-report, or export compatibility
surfaces consume the metadata. 2026-05-31 focused validation passed the version
contract (1) and the full static `plugin_tomls_declare` contract set (40) with
`--locked`, `--offline`, `--jobs 1`, and target dir
`D:\cargo-targets\zircon-plugin-bevy-refs`.

Static package identity is now tied to the package directory. Every static
`zircon_plugins/**/plugin.toml` top-level `id` must use a lowercase snake-style
token and match its enclosing `zircon_plugins/<plugin_id>/plugin.toml`
directory before plugin-window, catalog projection, or export selection accepts
the package. 2026-05-31 focused validation passed the package-identity contract
(1) and the full static `plugin_tomls_declare` contract set (41) with
`--locked`, `--offline`, `--jobs 1`, and target dir
`D:\cargo-targets\zircon-plugin-bevy-refs`.

Static plugin manifests now reject unknown top-level fields. Every static
`zircon_plugins/**/plugin.toml` key must belong to the known
`PluginPackageManifest` top-level field set before parser defaults,
plugin-window projection, or export selection can consume the file, so misspelled
metadata cannot be silently ignored. 2026-05-31 focused validation passed the
known-field contract (1) and the full static `plugin_tomls_declare` contract set
(42) with `--locked`, `--offline`, `--jobs 1`, and target dir
`D:\cargo-targets\zircon-plugin-bevy-refs`.

2026-05-31 focused validation passed the nested-field contract (1) and the full

Animation linked runtime metadata parity is now completed as the next narrow
plugin-ecosystem slice. Static `zircon_plugins/animation/plugin.toml`, linked

now all classify Animation as category `runtime`, maturity `beta`, and expose
partial status rows for `runtime.plugin.animation` plus

traceability on the owner capability. Focused red/green validation passed for
the linked package metadata. This is metadata/catalog parity only; M3 Animation
runtime semantics still require clip, graph, transition, event, fixture, and
profile evidence before promotion.

Navigation linked/static runtime metadata parity is now completed as another

linked `zircon_plugin_navigation_runtime::package_manifest()`, and the built-in
catalog now all classify Navigation as category `runtime`, maturity `beta`, and
`runtime.plugin.navigation` status `partial`, with the same note that gameplay
navmesh/pathfinding is separate from Bevy-style UI navigation parity. Focused
validation passed for the linked Navigation package metadata and for the static
TOML/catalog parity regression. The validation path first exposed an unrelated
asset-session type-bound compile break in `GpuModelResource`; the lower helper
was fixed and its focused fallback test passed before rerunning Navigation.
This is metadata/catalog parity only and does not promote gameplay navigation
or close the UI navigation split.

category for both descriptors, so descriptor-derived package manifests preserve
the static package classification. Focused red/green validation covered the
static TOML/catalog regression. This is metadata parity only and does not
promote VG/HGI render behavior or advanced quality-profile acceptance.

All static plugin manifests now have a checked-in public metadata guard.
`plugin_tomls_declare_public_package_metadata` recursively scans
`zircon_plugins/**/plugin.toml` and requires non-empty top-level `id`,
`version`, `sdk_api_version`, `display_name`, `category`, `description`,
`maturity`, non-empty `supported_targets`, and non-empty `capabilities`. This
turns the previous ad hoc metadata-gap scan into a runtime manifest regression
so future package additions cannot silently rely on parser defaults for
availability, plugin-window, or export-planning metadata.
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_package_ids` and
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_optional_feature_ids` now make static package ids
globally unique and reject optional-feature ids that collide with any package
or feature identity, so plugin-window, dependency, and export selection paths
do not inherit ambiguous manifest identities.
`plugin_tomls_declare_known_package_classification` constrains package
`category` to the current static package families and package `maturity` to the
known `PluginMaturity` TOML spellings.
`static_manifest_contracts::package_metadata::plugin_tomls_declare_public_display_strings_are_trimmed`
guards top-level package display strings, optional-feature display names, and
capability-status notes against leading/trailing whitespace before
plugin-window, readiness-report, runtime-report, or export diagnostics consume
public-facing text.
`plugin_tomls_declare_module_capabilities` extends the same static manifest
coverage to every `[[modules]]` and `[[optional_features.modules]]` row,
requiring non-empty string `capabilities` before module projection can feed the
loader, plugin-window, availability, or export layers.
`plugin_tomls_declare_module_target_modes` adds the matching module-row target
guard so package and optional-feature modules must also publish explicit
non-empty string `target_modes` instead of relying on parser-side defaults.
`plugin_tomls_declare_known_runtime_targets` constrains package
`supported_targets` and every module `target_modes` entry to
`client_runtime`, `server_runtime`, or `editor_host`, matching the current
`RuntimeTargetMode` TOML surface.
`static_manifest_contracts::modules::plugin_tomls_declare_module_targets_within_package_targets`
now requires every package and optional-feature module `target_modes` entry to
be covered by package-level `supported_targets`, so package availability cannot
be narrower than the module rows consumed by loader, plugin-window, or export
projection.
`static_manifest_contracts::modules::plugin_tomls_declare_editor_modules_target_editor_host_only`
now requires every `kind = "editor"` module row to advertise only `editor_host`,
so editor-only crates cannot leak into client/server runtime availability or
export projections.
`static_manifest_contracts::modules::plugin_tomls_declare_module_capabilities_match_module_kind`
now requires `runtime` module capabilities to stay under `runtime.*` and
`editor` module capabilities to stay under `editor.*`, so module-local
capability rows cannot be projected through the wrong runtime/editor host
surface.
`static_manifest_contracts::modules::plugin_tomls_declare_module_names_match_module_kind`
now requires runtime/editor module names to end with the matching `.runtime` or
`.editor` suffix, so module identity remains readable before loader,
plugin-window availability, and export projections consume it.
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_string_array_entries` rejects duplicate
`supported_targets`, `capabilities`, `default_packaging`, module `target_modes`,
and module/feature `capabilities` entries so repeated static metadata cannot
inflate availability, plugin-window, or export diagnostics.
`static_manifest_contracts::capabilities::plugin_tomls_declare_capability_namespaces`
now validates every capability-like static field in package capabilities,
capability statuses, package dependencies, optional-feature capabilities and
dependencies, module capabilities, importer required capabilities, and option
capability gates as trimmed lowercase dot-separated namespace segments.
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_capability_owners` gives each package-level and
optional-feature capability exactly one global static owner, while
`plugin_tomls_declare_capability_statuses_reference_owned_capabilities`
requires status rows to point back to capabilities declared by the same
manifest before readiness diagnostics consume them.
`static_manifest_contracts::capabilities::plugin_tomls_declare_capability_status_target_modes_within_package_targets`
requires every declared capability-status `target_modes` entry to be covered by
the package-level `supported_targets` before readiness diagnostics,
plugin-window availability, and export projections consume status metadata.
`plugin_tomls_declare_capability_status_rows` now guards
`[[capability_statuses]]` rows for non-empty capability ids, known
`CapabilityStatus` TOML spellings, manifest-local unique capability rows,
optional known/unique `target_modes`, unique `bevy_references`, and non-empty
notes before readiness diagnostics consume capability status metadata.
`plugin_tomls_declare_bevy_references_resolve_under_dev_bevy` also requires
each declared Bevy reference to be repository-relative, stay under `dev/bevy`,
avoid root/current/parent/drive-prefix path components, and resolve to an
existing file before package traceability metadata is accepted.
`static_manifest_contracts::event_catalogs::plugin_tomls_declare_event_catalog_namespaces_under_owner_package`
requires every event catalog namespace to stay under the declaring package id
before event reflection, plugin-window diagnostics, and export projections
consume event metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::event_catalogs::plugin_tomls_declare_event_payload_schemas_under_owner_package`
requires optional event payload schema ids to stay under the declaring package
id before event reflection, plugin-window diagnostics, and export projections
consume schema metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::options::plugin_tomls_declare_enum_option_defaults_are_tokens`
requires enum option `default_value` strings to stay in normalized lowercase
token form before editor configuration panels, runtime reports, plugin-window
diagnostics, and export projections consume option metadata.
The static manifest guards now live in
`zircon_runtime/src/tests/plugin_extensions/static_manifest_contracts.rs`; the
same module adds `plugin_tomls_declare_default_packaging_options`, requiring
non-empty string `default_packaging` for every static plugin package before
export planning consumes package metadata.
`plugin_tomls_declare_known_default_packaging_strategies` then constrains
package and optional-feature packaging entries to the known
`source_template`, `library_embed`, and `native_dynamic` export strategies.
`plugin_tomls_declare_package_dependencies` guards top-level
`[[dependencies]]` rows so package-level dependency declarations must publish
non-empty `id` and `capability` plus an explicit boolean `required` marker
before availability or editor/export diagnostics consume them.
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_package_dependency_rows` also rejects duplicate
top-level dependency `id`/`capability` pairs inside each static manifest.
`static_manifest_contracts::dependencies::plugin_tomls_declare_dependency_capabilities_reference_static_packages`
now resolves dependency rows that point at another static package id, requiring
their `capability` to be declared by the target package or one of its optional
features.
`plugin_tomls_declare_external_dependency_capabilities_use_host_namespaces`
keeps dependency rows without a static package target in explicit host-owned
`runtime.module.*` or `runtime.capability.*` namespaces.
`plugin_tomls_declare_optional_feature_metadata` extends that contract to
`[[optional_features]]`: feature rows must publish non-empty `id`,
`display_name`, `capabilities`, non-empty `default_packaging`, an explicit
boolean `enabled_by_default`, and `owner_plugin_id` matching the package id.
`plugin_tomls_declare_optional_feature_ids_under_owner_namespace` also requires
each optional feature id to stay under the owning package namespace.
`net` and `particles` now declare feature-level packaging explicitly for their
HTTP/WebSocket/RPC/replication/reliable-UDP/content-download and
physics/animation-control/GPU-simulation feature rows.
`plugin_tomls_declare_optional_feature_dependencies` now guards every
`[[optional_features.dependencies]]` row: each optional feature must declare at
least one dependency, every dependency must publish non-empty `plugin_id` and
`capability` plus boolean `primary`, and exactly one primary dependency must
point to the owner package id with a package-level capability.
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_optional_feature_dependency_rows` rejects duplicate
optional-feature dependency `plugin_id`/`capability` pairs before feature
selection and export planning consume the dependency set.
`plugin_tomls_declare_required_capability_gates_are_declared_or_host_owned`
guards option `required_capability` and importer `required_capabilities` gates,
requiring each gate to resolve to a declared static package/feature capability
or an explicitly host-owned capability before option panels or importer
selection consume it.
`plugin_tomls_declare_module_identity` and
`plugin_tomls_declare_unique_module_names` now guard every package and
optional-feature module row for non-empty `name`, known `kind`, non-empty
`crate_name`, and manifest-local unique module names before loader,
plugin-window, availability, and export projections consume module-local
metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::modules::plugin_tomls_declare_module_names_under_owner_namespace`
now requires package module names to stay under the package id namespace and
optional-feature module names to stay under the feature id namespace.
`plugin_tomls_declare_module_crates_in_plugin_workspace` parses
`zircon_plugins/Cargo.toml`, resolves every module `crate_name` to a real
workspace package `package.name`, and verifies the resolved member path stays
under the declaring package root before export generation or native/editor
module probing consumes the crate name.
`static_manifest_contracts::asset_importers::plugin_tomls_declare_asset_importer_rows`
now guards every static `[[asset_importers]]` row for globally unique importer
ids, matching owner `plugin_id`, integer priority, positive importer version,
known `ResourceKind` output names, normalized source-extension or full-suffix
selectors, and unique required capability rows before project import selection
and native fallback diagnostics consume importer metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::options::plugin_tomls_declare_option_rows` now
guards static `[[options]]` rows for globally unique lowercase dot-namespaced
keys, trimmed display/default strings, known option `value_type` spellings,
parseable typed defaults including lowercase enum default tokens, and non-empty
optional capability gates before editor configuration panels and runtime reports
consume option metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::event_catalogs::plugin_tomls_declare_event_catalog_rows`
now guards static `[[event_catalogs]]` rows for globally unique lowercase
dot-namespaced catalog namespaces, positive manifest versions, explicit
`events` arrays, lowercase namespace-scoped event ids, non-empty event display
names, and optional lowercase dot-namespaced payload schema ids under the
declaring package id with positive `vN` suffixes before event reflection and
runtime reports consume catalog metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::package_layout::plugin_tomls_declare_known_supported_platforms`
and `plugin_tomls_declare_package_root_arrays` now guard declared static
`supported_platforms` for known `ExportTargetPlatform` names and duplicate-free
entries, while declared `asset_roots` and `content_roots` must be unique,
relative, forward-slash package paths with no empty/current/parent segments
before export packaging and asset discovery consume package layout metadata.
`static_manifest_contracts::package_coordinates::plugin_tomls_declare_package_coordinates`
and `plugin_tomls_declare_unique_resolved_package_ids` now guard optional
reverse-DNS package coordinates as an all-or-none set (`package_prefix`,
`package_company`, and `package_name`) with trimmed lowercase coordinate
segments, then prove the resolved `PluginPackageManifest::package_id()` values
are globally unique before registry and export report surfaces consume them.
Focused validation passed for the 58-test `plugin_tomls_declare` static
manifest contract set through the Cargo wrapper with `--locked`, `--offline`,

Cargo acceptance remains pending.

capability-status target coverage guard, event-catalog namespace ownership

and optional-feature/module/dependency-provider/importer/option/event/

#### Validation Outcome Classification

Use this table when recording any queued proof. The classification decides whether a lane can move upward, stays deferred, or must hand the failure to another owner.

| Outcome | Required record | Evidence state effect | Next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Focused proof passed | Exact command, date/time, `--locked` status, target dir/profile, owner note, and relevant pass count or check summary. | May move only the named slice to `Focused Accepted`; it does not promote adjacent slices or the whole lane. | Update the owner note/docs, then decide the next queue item for that same lane. |
| Owner-lane test failed | Exact failing command, first failing test or Rust diagnostic, owning source/test path, and whether the failure matches the active owner scope. | Keep the lane at `Blocked / Deferred` or lower accepted state; do not broaden acceptance. | Route to the active owner note and stop upper promotion. |
| Lower-layer compile failure before target tests | First compile diagnostic, crate/module path, likely owner lane, and confirmation that intended focused tests did not run. | Does not count as focused failure for the target lane and cannot be used as acceptance. | Record blocker against the lower owner and retry only after that owner resolves or defers it. |
| Timeout during dependency compilation or active queue saturation | Command, timeout point, process count, target dir/profile, and whether any Rust diagnostic appeared. | Stays `Blocked / Deferred`; timeout is not a pass and not necessarily a Rust failure. | Wait for a quiet window or owner permission before retrying the same command. |
| Queue preempted by another owner lane | Current queue item, newly active owner process/note, and the paused command if any. | No evidence-state change. | Refresh coordination and either wait or update the active validation queue. |
| Stale ledger or replaced owner note | Which plan/note is newer than the ledger or which listed note closed/archived. | No acceptance command may start; only routing/deferred state can be recorded. | Refresh `Promotion Pause Ledger` and `Active Validation Queue` before retrying. |

### Evidence State Vocabulary

Use these labels consistently when updating this roadmap, session notes, and module docs. A lane can only move upward when the required evidence was produced in the current checkout or the exact checkout/target/profile is recorded with the acceptance note.

| Evidence state | Allowed claim | Required evidence | Not enough |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Planned | Scope, ordering, owner lane, Bevy/Zircon anchors, and first validation gate are written down. | Roadmap/docs entry names source/test anchors, failure routing, and quiet-window command shape. | Static source exists; old plan says the feature should exist. |
| Implemented / Static Evidence | Source/docs/tests appear structurally complete, but Cargo acceptance is not fresh. | Source scan, docs update, rustfmt where touched, `git diff --check`, whitespace/conflict scans, and active owner note if the lane is occupied. | Claiming the milestone is accepted; using screenshot, render smoke, or old Cargo logs. |
| Focused Accepted | The narrow lane claim is fresh for the current checkout. | Exact `cargo test` or `cargo check` focused filter with `--locked` unless lockfile work is in scope; pass/fail summary recorded in the owning note/docs. | A broader command that failed before reaching the focused tests; unrelated package tests. |
| Lane Accepted | The whole lane's scoped suite passed after focused blockers were resolved. | Focused gates plus lane suite, for example `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib <lane-filter> --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`, and docs/session evidence ledger updated. | One submilestone test; tests passed in another active session without recorded command/date/target. |
| Package Accepted | Package-level validator or equivalent package build/test passed. | `validate-matrix.ps1 -Package zircon_runtime` / `-Package zircon_app` or equivalent exact package commands, with target dir/profile and `--locked` status recorded. | Focused tests only; package command blocked by another lane compile error. |
| CI-Parity Accepted | Full workspace and plugin CI shape is fresh or the exact CI-equivalent command set is recorded. | Full `validate-matrix.ps1` or CI-equivalent `cargo build/test --workspace --locked --verbose` plus plugin workspace check/build/test, with lower-lane failures absent or explicitly deferred by owner. | M12/full validator skipped owner-lane blockers; advanced/Solari/render smoke replacing asset/UI/platform evidence. |
| Blocked / Deferred | The lane cannot be promoted without another owner finishing or fixing a lower shared layer. | Exact failing command or queue condition, first failing owner lane, owning session/doc link, and next retry gate. | Silent deferral; broad "workspace dirty" note without owner or command shape. |

Status wording should not collapse these levels. For example, M78-M82 with source-contract and whitespace evidence remain `Implemented / Static Evidence` until their focused Cargo guards pass; M10 advanced/Solari can be `Focused Accepted` for advanced renderer gates while M10W CI-parity remains `Blocked / Deferred` by UI/asset failures.

### Quiet-Window Operating Rules

Use these rules before running any command from the promotion queue. They are intentionally stricter than ordinary local development because this checkout often has multiple active Codex sessions sharing the same Cargo/rustc resources.

| Rule | Required action | Stop condition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Refresh ownership first | Run the recent coordination scan and read any session note that owns the target lane or a likely lower-layer failure. For M9 scene gates, also refresh ECS ownership; for M10 render gates, refresh asset/UI/material ownership; for M12/full validator, refresh every active lane. | If another active note owns the failing source/test area, do not patch it from the roadmap lane. Route the failure to that note. |
| Refresh pause ledger | Before Layer 1 package validators, M10W, M12, or full workspace validators, compare the `Promotion Pause Ledger` timestamp with current owner plans/notes for the target lane. If any listed owner is newer, closed, archived, or replaced, update the ledger first. | If the ledger is stale, only record `Blocked / Deferred` routing; do not start an upper acceptance command. |
| Check active validation queue | Read `Active Validation Queue` before starting any queued proof and run only the first unresolved owner-lane command for that lane. | If a queued proof has not run, do not skip to package/full validation. If a different owner lane starts compiling, record the queue condition and wait. |
| Classify every result | After any queued command attempt, classify it with `Validation Outcome Classification` before changing evidence state. | If the result is unclassified, leave the roadmap state unchanged and write the missing details first. |
| Confirm queue quietness | Check active `cargo`/`rustc`/`cl`/link processes before launching validation. A quiet window means no active process from unrelated Zircon owner lanes, or the owning note explicitly permits the run. | If unrelated compile jobs are active or new jobs keep entering, record `Blocked / Deferred` with the intended command and retry gate. |
| Start with the narrowest proof | Run the first focused gate named by the lane, normally one `cargo test -p ... --lib <filter> --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` or a package-specific focused command. | If compilation fails before reaching the intended tests, do not infer lane failure; identify the first compile owner and stop. |
| Escalate one rung at a time | Focused Accepted -> Lane Accepted -> Package Accepted -> CI-Parity Accepted. Do not jump from static evidence to full validator. | If a narrower rung is missing fresh evidence, do not run or claim a broader rung as acceptance. |
| Preserve `--locked` | Keep `--locked` on validation commands unless lockfile work is explicitly the lane's scope. | If `--locked` fails because lockfile update is needed, record the lockfile blocker and route it to the owning workspace/manifests lane. |
| Record timeout honestly | A timeout during dependency compilation is not a Rust test failure. Record command, timeout point, active queue context, target dir/profile if known, and whether any Rust diagnostic appeared. | Do not mark focused acceptance from a timeout; do not repeat the same long command while unrelated compile saturation remains. |
| Use target dirs deliberately | Prefer the repo validator's shared target-dir policy or the lane's existing target dir. Do not mint unlimited new target directories during retries. | If target-dir free space or slot ownership is unclear, stop and use the validation policy before running broad Cargo. |
| Write evidence back | Every successful or blocked promotion attempt must update the owning session note or docs with exact command, date, target/profile, `--locked` status, and pass/fail summary. | If no evidence ledger is written, the roadmap status must remain at its previous evidence state. |

### Layer 2 Promotion Queue - Quiet Window Order

When the shared Cargo queue is quiet, promote Layer 2 in this order. Each row starts with the narrowest gate that can prove the lane's current claim; only expand after that gate is fresh in the current checkout.

| Order | Lane | First quiet-window gate | Expand only after | Failure routing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | M7 Platform / Window / Input | Platform plan now records M5/M6 accepted evidence, and the plan-only Platform reading guide/evidence index are archived/completed. Refresh Platform validation ownership before starting M78-M82 focused guards, M7 platform suite, app-entry, or package gates. | Run `cargo test -p zircon_runtime platform --lib --no-default-features --features core-min --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`, then scoped `zircon_app runtime_entry` guards and package validators only after refreshed validation owner accepts the next gate. | Failures in platform/input/app-entry/export/profile-feature validators route to the refreshed M7 validation owner; ECS/asset/UI/material/Hub/Sound compile blockers route to their fresh owner notes first. |
| 2 | M8 Asset / Hot Reload | Refresh asset/texture ownership after archived KTX2 KVD min-length, invalid-UTF8, padding-range, multiple-records, KVD record-sequence, archived DDS DX10 short-header, archived DDS short-header, DDS magic, DDS header-size, DDS pixel-format-size, DDS primary-layout flags, DDS luminance-mask, DDS alpha-pixels, DDS alpha-mask, DDS uncompressed fixture, DDS uncompressed masks, DDS depth flag, DDS DX10 format, DDS pitch/linear-size, DDS pixel-format/storage-shape, KTX2 index offsets, KTX2 KVD records, KTX2 Zstd/ZLIB SGD, BasisLZ, KTX2 SGD-none, DDS DX10 cubemap caps, ASTC footprint, KTX mip-count, KTX2 metadata-level/metadata/level-overlap, DDS mip-count/pixel-format/payload, KTX1/KTX2/DDS/container Cargo deferrals, KTX2 level-index, mesh, and importer capability waits. | Expand back to adjacent texture readiness (`texture_upload_readiness_rejects_short_ktx_level_declarations`) and NativeDynamic/importer roundtrip filters only after archived KTX/DDS/ASTC/container readiness, KTX2 level-index, mesh, and service-boundary results are recorded or explicitly deferred under a refreshed asset owner. | Do not repair asset readiness from M10 render or M7 platform lanes; route asset/texture failures to the refreshed asset owner unless that owner explicitly narrows them elsewhere. |
| 3 | M9 Scene / World / Authoring Runtime | Refresh ECS ownership first; historical event-writer batch count is focused accepted, but query-combinations and scene-authoring gates remain unresolved and no fresh ECS owner remains inside the 4h window. | Expand to `ecs_query_combinations`, then `scene::tests::dynamic_scene`, `scene::tests::asset_scene`, `scene::tests::editor_projection`, `scene::tests::ecs_reflect`, then full `scene::tests` only after ECS query helpers are fresh. | ECS/world failures route to the refreshed ECS note; asset dependency failures route to the active/refreshed asset note; render extract failures require refreshed M10 ownership. |

Then rerun or defer package-boundary validation, timeline/runtime-plugin validation, and recover or refresh Sound ownership for dynamic-events, HRTF profile, source seek, output-render, mixer-presets, playback-controls, source-controls, playback-status, source-status, clip-assets, output-device, and older service-lifecycle gaps. | Expand to sound editor/live-output checks and profile/provider availability only after package-boundary validation plus timeline/runtime-plugin validation, dynamic-events, HRTF profile, source seek, output-render, mixer-presets, playback-controls, source-controls, playback-status, source-status, clip-assets, output-device, and service lifecycle slices are recovered and close or explicitly defer. | Sound framework/runtime/editor files stay in the Sound owner lane; new Material reporting/export failures route to refreshed Material ownership. |

Treat archived ZUI child mount 24/0, control-id trim 23/0, component-name, self-import, support/single-root, control-id/node/header/asset-id evidence as scoped focused unless broader `.zui` gates fail. | Expand to layout deterministic, widget state/event, focus traversal, accessibility snapshot, and UI + scene picking interop gates only after active/refreshed/archived UI slices close or defer and `.zui` asset-management evidence is refreshed where needed. | UI/MUI/Text/Input/A11y failures route to the refreshed UI owner; `.zui` support/control-id/header/node/asset-management failures route to the active/refreshed ZUI owner; graphics/material compile blockers route to the material/render owner before broad M11 promotion. |

Promotion evidence must be written back to either the active session note or the owning docs page before claiming acceptance. If a quiet-window command fails before reaching the intended tests because another lane cannot compile, record that as a blocker and stop that lane's promotion instead of modifying unrelated code.

M46 mouse wheel 能力声明把 `platform.mouse_wheel` 加入同一诊断面，依据是 Bevy `MouseWheel` / `MouseScrollUnit::{Line, Pixel}` 词汇 [mouse.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_input/src/mouse.rs:154) 与 winit `WindowEvent::MouseWheel` 的 LineDelta / PixelDelta 转发路径 [state.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_winit/src/state.rs:338)。Zircon runtime-preview 已把滚轮事件归约为带单位的 `MouseWheelEvent`，当前切片只补 platform matrix 与 app diagnostics，不改变 UI scroll/picking ownership。

M48 touch event 能力声明把 `platform.touch_events` 加入同一诊断面，依据是 Bevy `TouchInput` / `TouchPhase` / `Touches` 词汇 [touch.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_input/src/touch.rs:53)、winit touch 转换 [converters.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_winit/src/converters.rs:52) 与 `WindowEvent::Touch` 转发路径 [state.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_winit/src/state.rs:356)。Zircon runtime-preview 已有 touch ABI 与 `TouchPoint` 归约，当前切片只补 platform matrix 与 app diagnostics，不改变 UI picking/a11y ownership。

M49 mouse button 能力声明把 `platform.mouse_buttons` 加入同一诊断面，依据是 Bevy `MouseButtonInput` / `MouseButton` / `ButtonState` 词汇 [mouse.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_input/src/mouse.rs:42)、winit mouse button 与 element state 转换 [converters.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_winit/src/converters.rs:33) 和 `WindowEvent::MouseInput` 转发路径 [state.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_winit/src/state.rs:316)。Zircon runtime-preview 已有 mouse button ABI 与 `ButtonInputState` 归约，当前切片只补 platform matrix 与 app diagnostics，不改变 UI click/picking ownership。

M50 window-attribute family modularization 把 `runtime_entry_app/window_attributes.rs` 收束为 folder-backed `runtime_entry_app/window_attributes/`，依据是 Bevy 将窗口 DTO 词汇留在 `bevy_window/src/window.rs`，并把 monitor/video-mode 到 winit 的解析放在 `bevy_winit/src/winit_windows.rs` 与运行期更新放在 `bevy_winit/src/system.rs`。Zircon 只复制这种主机边界：`builder.rs` 组装 `WindowAttributes`，`monitor.rs` 捕获 active event-loop monitor inventory，`position.rs` 负责 centered/explicit placement，`fullscreen.rs` 负责 borderless/exclusive fallback，`video_mode.rs` 负责 current/specific matching；外部仍只通过 `runtime_window_attributes(...)` 被 `window_creation.rs` 消费。

Zircon 落地项：

- `PlatformModule` 和 `InputModule` 默认纳入 `DefaultPlugins`，但 `MinimalPlugins` 只保留 headless-safe 的 time/tasks/diagnostics。
- `PlatformCapabilityReport` 覆盖 desktop/mobile/browser/headless 的 window、monitor inventory、window events、window lifecycle、window metrics、IME、keyboard events、cursor boundary、cursor options、mouse buttons、mouse wheel、touch events、gesture events、pointer position、raw mouse motion、input、gamepad、gamepad events、gamepad rumble、file drag/drop、cursor、scale factor、raw-device motion、lifecycle 支持矩阵。
- `RuntimeEntryApp` 补齐 winit 事件映射：scale factor 独立状态、IME composition/delete-surrounding、cursor options、focused/occluded/theme/moved/close/destroyed、file drag/drop、raw device motion、touch、keyboard、mouse wheel x/y + Line/Pixel unit、mouse、gamepad；共享 UI dispatch host request 侧补齐 cursor/composition geometry 与 surrounding-text DTO，后续收口 native host consumption。
- 输入状态从事件流稳定归约成 frame snapshot：button just-pressed/just-released、轴/滚轮 x/y 与单位累计、legacy scalar wheel accumulator、IME composition、window status、cursor-inside-window、file drag/drop 队列。
- 接受标准：平台能力矩阵测试、`platform.monitor_inventory` / `platform.window_events` / `platform.window_lifecycle` / `platform.window_metrics` / `platform.ime` / `platform.keyboard_events` / `platform.cursor_boundary` / `platform.cursor_options` / `platform.mouse_buttons` / `platform.mouse_wheel` / `platform.touch_events` / `platform.gesture_events` / `platform.pointer_position` / `platform.raw_mouse_motion` / `platform.gamepad_events` / `platform.gamepad_rumble` app diagnostics、runtime ABI event coverage、runtime preview host source guard、app runner smoke test、headless profile 不创建窗口。
- M46 接受标准追加：`platform.mouse_wheel` app diagnostics、runtime prelude export、desktop/mobile winit 支持声明、browser/headless unavailable 声明、Bevy `MouseWheel` / `MouseScrollUnit` 与 `bevy_winit::state` 引用齐备。
- M48 接受标准追加：`platform.touch_events` app diagnostics、runtime prelude export、desktop/mobile winit 支持声明、browser/headless unavailable 声明、Bevy `TouchInput` / `TouchPhase` / `Touches`、`bevy_winit::converters` 与 `bevy_winit::state` 引用齐备。
- M49 接受标准追加：`platform.mouse_buttons` app diagnostics、runtime prelude export、desktop/mobile winit 支持声明、browser/headless unavailable 声明、Bevy `MouseButtonInput` / `MouseButton` / `ButtonState`、`bevy_winit::converters` 与 `bevy_winit::state` 引用齐备。
- M50 接受标准追加：`runtime_entry_app/window_attributes/` folder-backed source guard、旧 `window_attributes.rs` absent guard、root structural re-export、builder/monitor/position/fullscreen/video-mode helper family coverage、runtime-present 与 runtime framework window docs 同步，且不改变窗口创建调用面。
- M51 接受标准追加：`platform.keyboard_events` app diagnostics、runtime prelude export、desktop/mobile winit 支持声明、browser/headless unavailable 声明、Bevy `KeyboardInput` / `KeyboardFocusLost` / `KeyCode` / `Key`、`bevy_winit::converters` 与 `bevy_winit::state` 引用齐备。
- M52 接受标准追加：`runtime_entry_app/gamepad/` folder-backed source guard、旧 `gamepad.rs` absent guard、root structural wiring、host/polling/events/codes helper family coverage、runtime-present docs 同步，且不改变 optional `gamepad-gilrs` feature gate、`create_gilrs()` 或 `poll_gamepads(...)` 调用面。

M7 剩余收口顺序固定为测试阶段而非新功能扩张：先刷新 platform/input ownership 并确认 Cargo 队列状态；若队列仍忙，先跑低干扰 real Cargo-driven `-RunProfileFeatureContract -ProfileFeatureContractLabel "zircon_runtime target-server"`；队列安静后再跑 full real Cargo-driven `-RunExportPlatformContract`、full `-RunProfileFeatureContract` 和 broader M5 testing-stage commands。随后依次 fresh rerun M78 `platform::tests::headless_synthetic_input`、M79 `platform::tests::status_semantics`、M80 `platform::tests::diagnostic_status_consistency`、M81 `platform::tests::diagnostic_metadata`、M82 `platform::tests::app_feature_manifest`，命令形态保持 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib --no-default-features --features core-min --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` 的 focused guard gate；再跑 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime platform --lib --no-default-features --features core-min --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` 作为 M7 platform suite gate；涉及 app-host guard 时追加 `cargo test -p zircon_app runtime_entry --lib --no-default-features --features platform-x11,platform-wayland,input-mouse,input-keyboard,input-touch --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`。只有这些当前 checkout fresh checks 通过后，才能把 M7 从“源码/文档/静态 guard 完成”提升为“测试阶段接受”；否则继续记录为实现期证据，不能用旧日志、其它 lane 的测试或 M10 render validator 替代。

### M8 Asset / Hot Reload / Import Pipeline

目标是让默认应用拥有 Bevy 式 “路径加载、句柄引用、依赖就绪、开发期热更新” 体验，但落地到 Zircon 现有 `asset` / `zmeta` / importer 架构。Bevy 的基准是 `AssetPlugin` 的默认 `assets` 与 `imported_assets/Default` 路径、`watch_for_changes_override`、`AssetMode`、`AssetMetaCheck`、`UnapprovedPathMode`、`AssetServer` 与 `AssetLoader` 注册 [bevy_asset/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_asset/src/lib.rs:153)、[bevy_asset/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_asset/src/lib.rs:302)，以及公开文档 [AssetPlugin](https://docs.rs/bevy/latest/bevy/asset/struct.AssetPlugin.html)。

Zircon 落地项：

- `AssetModule` 纳入 `DefaultPlugins`，`DevPlugins` 打开 watch/hot-reload profile，`HeadlessPlugins` 可禁用 watcher 但保留 registry 与 importer。
- 统一 `AssetReference`、project URI、source path、`.zmeta` sidecar、package asset registry、runtime asset handles 的状态模型。
- importer 需要对 texture、shader、material、scene、animation 等默认资产类型给出能力报告、错误诊断和依赖列表。
- 热更新要从文件 watcher 到 importer、artifact store、runtime resource residency、render resource invalidation 形成闭环。
- 接受标准：source URI/meta roundtrip、unapproved path rejection、watch change reload、missing dependency diagnostic、runtime scene/material/texture smoke tests。

#### M8 子里程碑执行表

M8 的验收不能只看单个 importer 成功，也不能用 render fallback 或 editor asset browser 可见性替代 runtime asset contract。Bevy 的关键模型是 `Handle`/`Assets<T>`、`LoadState`、依赖加载状态、`AssetLoader`、watch reload 和 typed asset events；Zircon 的落点已经分散在 `zircon_runtime::asset::facade`、`asset::project`、`asset::importer`、`asset::watch`、`asset::pipeline` 和 `asset::assets::*`，所以 M8 需要按下列顺序收口：

| M8 子里程碑 | Zircon 落点 | 实现切片 | 测试阶段与验收证据 | 不接受的替代证据 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M8.1 Default asset profile 与模块边界 | `zircon_app::plugins`、`zircon_runtime::asset::module`、`AssetModule`。 | 固定 `DefaultPlugins`/`DevPlugins`/`HeadlessPlugins` 中 asset/watch/importer 的包含与禁用语义；Dev 打开 watch profile，Headless 保留 registry/importer 但不强制 watcher。 | app plugin/profile diagnostics、asset module registration guards、headless watcher-disabled checks。 | editor 能浏览资源；render 能画内建 fallback。 |
| M8.2 Typed facade / handle / load state | `zircon_runtime::asset::facade::{Asset, Handle, Assets, AssetEvent, AssetLoadStates}`。 | typed handle kind、event filtering、root/direct/recursive dependency state、last-good payload 和 read-only state query 继续收束到 facade。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib asset::tests::facade --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`；docs 更新 `docs/zircon_runtime/asset/facade.md`。 | 旧 resource handle API 可用；只检查 raw `ResourceState`。 |
| M8.3 Project URI / `.zmeta` / package registry | `asset::project::{paths, meta, manifest, package_asset_registry}`。 | 固定 source URI、sidecar UUID、subasset entry UUID、package asset registry、unapproved path policy 和 source-hash/importer-version 记录。 | `asset::tests::project::{uri,zmeta,manifest,package_assets}` focused filters；source URI/meta roundtrip 和 forbidden path diagnostics。 | 根据路径字符串临时派生 ID；保留旧 `*.meta.toml` 作为 runtime identity。 |
| M8.4 Importer registry / plugin contract | `asset::importer::{contract, registry, native, ingest}`、`zircon_plugins::asset_importers`。 | full-suffix 优先、priority 覆盖、required capabilities、native envelope failure、wrong output kind、单文件失败继续扫描。 | `asset::tests::{registry,assets::importer,pipeline::manager}` 和 plugin importer workspace gates。 | 在 project scan 里继续按扩展名内建分支；未知 typed TOML 静默当 data。 |
| M8.5 Default asset families | `asset::assets::{texture,mesh,shader,material,scene,animation,font,sound,ui}`。 | texture container/upload readiness、mesh first-class attributes、shader imports/defs/readiness、material dependency set、scene asset dependency collection 分别有结构化 descriptor。 | `asset::tests::assets::{texture_importer,mesh,shader_readiness,material,scene,render_product}` focused filters。 | 单个 PNG/OBJ/WGSL smoke；render product 自动 fallback 成功。 |
| M8.6 Dependency readiness / diagnostics | `asset::facade::readiness`、`asset::assets::*::validation`、`asset::pipeline::manager::records`。 | 把 missing dependency、short/invalid texture payload、shader import redirect、material texture dependency、failed recursive dependency 都落成 machine-readable readiness report。 | 先跑当前 blocker `texture_upload_readiness_rejects_ktx2_level_payload_overlapping_index`，再跑 `texture_upload_readiness_rejects_short_ktx_level_declarations` 和 adjacent readiness filters。 | 字符串错误、panic、或只返回 `Failed` 不解释 dependency path。 |
| M8.7 Watch / hot reload / artifact residency | `asset::watch`、`asset::artifact`、`asset::pipeline::{worker_pool,manager}`。 | watch event fold、source change reimport、artifact cache key、runtime payload store、reload failure keep-last-good、removed resource cleanup。 | `asset::tests::{watcher,artifact,pipeline::worker_pool,pipeline::manager}` focused filters。 | 手动 reload path 通过；重新启动后资源能加载。 |
| M8.8 Render prepare handoff / docs acceptance | `asset::assets::render_product`、`zircon_runtime::graphics::scene::resources`、`docs/zircon_runtime/asset/*`。 | 只定义 asset-to-render readiness handoff：PreparedTexture/Mesh/Shader/Material 的 cache invalidation 与 last-good ownership 交给 M10/render lane。 | `asset::tests::assets::render_product` fresh 后，再由 M10 跑 render product submit gates；docs 记录 Bevy 对照和 Zircon divergence。 | 从 render lane 修 asset readiness；用 M10 advanced/Solari 结果关闭资产缺口。 |

M8 quiet-window 测试阶段顺序：先刷新 asset ownership，并处理已归档 importer capability service slice 的 focused behavior timeout；若 `asset_manager_service_reports_importer_capabilities_before_and_after_project_open` fresh 通过或被 owner 明确 deferred，再跑 `git diff --check` 与路线图/asset docs whitespace scan；然后按 M8.2-M8.7 的 focused filters 逐项执行。只有当前 service-boundary gate、texture readiness blocker 和 adjacent readiness filters 都 fresh 通过后，才跑 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib asset::tests --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`；最后再进入 `cargo test --manifest-path zircon_plugins/Cargo.toml --locked --jobs 1` 和 workspace validator。若在编译阶段先撞到 UI/render/ECS/platform 错误，记录 blocker 并回到对应 active note，不从 asset session 外部抢修。

### M9 Scene / World / Authoring Runtime

目标是把 Zircon scene/world 从“能加载”推进到 Bevy `ScenePlugin` 式可组合、可补丁、可等待依赖的 authoring runtime。Bevy 的基准是 `Scene` / `ScenePatch` / `SceneListPatch`、queued spawn、template patching、asset-backed prefab 和测试 app 组合 [bevy_scene/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_scene/src/lib.rs:1)、[bevy_scene/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_scene/src/lib.rs:539)。

Zircon 落地项：

- `SceneModule` 纳入 `DefaultPlugins`，依赖 `AssetModule`、reflection、world hierarchy、typed/dynamic scene values。
- `dynamic_scene` 支持文档化 schema、patch/remap、entity name/reference、component lifecycle、asset reference dependency collection。
- `World` / scene runtime 支持 queued scene instantiation：依赖未 ready 时排队，依赖 ready 后 resolve/spawn，并给出失败诊断。
- editor projection 可以从同一 scene model 生成 hierarchy/inspector 视图，不另造编辑器专用格式。
- 接受标准：scene document parse/serialize roundtrip、patch merge、entity remap、asset dependency wait、editor projection smoke tests。

#### M9 子里程碑执行表

M9 夹在 asset、ECS/reflect、render extract 和 editor projection 之间，必须明确所有权：scene/world authoring 只定义 runtime scene contract，不借机改 asset importer、render submission 或 editor UI。当前源码已有 `zircon_runtime::scene::{dynamic_scene,ecs,reflect,editor_projection,world}` 和 `zircon_runtime::asset::assets::scene` 锚点，M9 按下面的顺序推进：

| M9 子里程碑 | Zircon 落点 | 实现切片 | 测试阶段与验收证据 | 不接受的替代证据 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M9.1 SceneModule / profile boundary | `zircon_runtime::scene::SceneModule`、`zircon_app::plugins`、`load_level_asset`。 | 固定 `SceneModule` 在 `DefaultPlugins` 中依赖 asset/reflect/world，但不直接依赖 renderer；Headless 可加载 scene authoring data。 | module registration/profile diagnostics、`scene_boundary` guards、app default-profile checks。 | editor 能打开 hierarchy；render extract 能读当前 level。 |
| M9.2 Dynamic scene schema / reflected values | `scene::dynamic_scene`、`scene::serializer`、`scene::reflect`。 | `DynamicScene`、`DynamicEntity`、`DynamicComponent`、`DynamicResource`、scene value conversion、missing type diagnostics 和 schema version 固化。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib scene::tests::dynamic_scene --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`，再跑 `scene::tests::ecs_reflect` focused filters。 | JSON roundtrip 只覆盖固定组件；unknown type 静默丢失。 |
| M9.3 World / ECS authority for authoring | `scene::world`、`scene::ecs`、`scene::reflect::WorldReflection`。 | 保持 `World` 是唯一 authority；typed/dynamic/reflected components 走同一 registry/storage/projection，不让 editor 或 asset scene 另建状态。 | `scene::tests::{world_basics,ecs_typed_api,ecs_systems,ecs_schedule,ecs_change_detection}`；失败先读 active ECS note。 | 绕过 `World` 直接改 level records；为 editor 维护第二套 component store。 |
| M9.4 Asset-backed scene loading / dependency wait | `asset::assets::scene`、`scene::module::load_level_asset`、`scene::tests::asset_scene`。 | Scene asset 解析收集 asset references；依赖未 ready 时排队或给出 structured blocked status；依赖 ready 后 resolve/spawn。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib scene::tests::asset_scene --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`，并与 M8 dependency readiness 结果交叉记录。 | 直接同步加载所有依赖；缺资源时 spawn fallback entity 且不诊断。 |
| M9.5 Patch / remap / migration | `scene::dynamic_scene::{patch,remap}`、`scene::world::property_access`。 | ScenePatch、EntityRemap、property path write/read、old scene document migration 和 partial apply failure rollback 规则文档化。 | `scene::tests::{dynamic_scene,property_paths,asset_scene}` focused filters；old/new scene fixtures roundtrip。 | 只做 full document replace；patch 失败后留下半应用 world。 |
| M9.6 Editor projection contract | `scene::editor_projection::{hierarchy,inspector,projection}`。 | hierarchy rows、inspector fields、selection/undo command surface 只消费 runtime projections，不把 editor UI state 写回 world storage。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib scene::tests::editor_projection --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`；editor UI tests 另属 M11。 | editor 专用 scene format；从 UI widget state 反推 runtime component shape。 |
| M9.7 Render extract / picking boundary | `scene::render_extract`、`core::framework::render::scene_extract`、`graphics::scene`。 | Scene 只产出 render extract DTO 和 picking-visible semantics；presentation、material fallback、UI picking 判定归 M10/M11。 | `scene::tests::asset_scene` + M10 render extract focused gates after render lane confirms ownership。 | 用 renderer 能显示 scene 关闭 scene schema/patch/reflect 缺口。 |
| M9.8 Docs / acceptance ledger | `docs/zircon_runtime/scene/{dynamic_scene,ecs,reflect,editor_projection,render_extract}.md`。 | 每个子里程碑记录 Bevy 对照、Zircon divergence、source/test anchors、quiet-window evidence 和 active-owner routing。 | docs diff hygiene、focused scene tests、再跑 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib scene::tests --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`。 | 旧 ECS 日志、M10 render validator、或 editor smoke 替代 scene authoring acceptance。 |

M9 quiet-window 测试阶段顺序：先刷新 ECS/scene ownership（截至 2026-05-27 01:33 没有 fresh M9/ECS active note），并确认 asset/UI/material 活跃 slices 不会前置阻塞；再依次跑 `scene::tests::dynamic_scene`、`scene::tests::ecs_reflect`、`scene::tests::asset_scene`、`scene::tests::editor_projection`、`scene::tests::world_basics` focused filters；只有这些 fresh 通过后再跑 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib scene::tests --locked --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`。如果失败位于 ECS system/message/change-detection support 层，按 support-first 规则回到 ECS owner；如果失败位于 asset dependency readiness，回到 M8/asset owner；如果失败位于 render extract consumption，回到 M10/render owner。

### M10 Render / Core Pipeline / Presentation

目标是让 Zircon `DefaultPlugins` 的渲染体验接近 Bevy “默认窗口 + render app + extract/prepare/queue/render + diagnostics” 的开发完成度。Bevy 的基准是默认组合中的 `RenderPlugin`、`ImagePlugin`、`MeshPlugin`、`CameraPlugin`、`LightPlugin`、`PipelinedRenderingPlugin`、`CorePipelinePlugin`、`PostProcessPlugin`、PBR/UI render 顺序 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:32)，`RenderPlugin` 的 RenderApp/SubApp、`RenderSystems`、GPU resource init、pipelined rendering 入口 [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:96)，以及 render diagnostics recorder [bevy_render/src/diagnostic/mod.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/diagnostic/mod.rs:28)。

Zircon 落地项：

- `RenderModule`、`ImageModule`、`MeshModule`、`CameraModule`、`LightModule`、`PostProcessModule`、`UiRenderModule` 按依赖顺序纳入 `DefaultPlugins`。
- 保持 `zircon_runtime::graphics` 的 framework/backend 分层：extract runtime frame、prepare GPU resources、queue render product、submit/present，不把 editor viewport 特例塞进 runtime pipeline。
- render diagnostics 记录 CPU/GPU pass timing、render asset bytes、pipeline/cache status、viewport/present errors，并能被 `DiagnosticStoreLogSchedule` 或 overlay 消费。
- 支持 headless/offscreen capture、runtime preview window present、editor viewport present 三种 presentation surface。
- 接受标准：render module dependency ordering、headless no-present path、basic camera/mesh/material submit、post-process stack validation、render diagnostic snapshot、runtime/editor presentation smoke tests。

#### M10 子里程碑执行表

M10 不能用 Virtual Geometry、Hybrid GI 或 Solari 的强能力替代基础渲染产品面。Bevy 文档把 high-level profile 和 renderer collection 分开：`default` 是 `2d + 3d + ui + audio` [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:22)，`common_api` 明确不含实际 renderer [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:46)，`2d_bevy_render` / `3d_bevy_render` / `ui_bevy_render` 才是内置 renderer collection [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:48)。因此 Zircon M10 按以下子里程碑推进，每一项都要先有实现切片，再进入测试阶段，不能用高级渲染通过来关闭默认 2D/3D/UI 缺口。

| M10 子里程碑 | Bevy 源码与文档依据 | Zircon 落点 | 实现切片 | 测试阶段与验收证据 | 不接受的替代证据 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M10.1 DefaultRender profile 与模块顺序 | `DefaultPlugins` 的 render-facing 顺序见 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:43)，profile/collection 分层见 [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:22)。 | `zircon_runtime::core::framework::render::profile`、`zircon_app::EntryConfig`、`BuiltinEngineEntry`。 | 固定 `DefaultRender = CommonRenderApi + Render2d + Render3d + Ui`；记录模块顺序；确保 `Headless` 不携带 render product；`AdvancedRender` / `SolariExperimental` 只能后置。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime render_profile --locked`、`cargo check -p zircon_app --locked --all-targets`；文档验收见 [profile.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/docs/zircon_runtime/core/framework/render/profile.md)。 | VG/HGI/Solari provider 注册成功；单个 renderer smoke test。 |
| M10.2 Render app / schedule / render graph | Bevy `RenderPlugin` 使用 render `SubApp` 并可由 `PipelinedRenderingPlugin` 并行执行 [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:120)，`RenderSystems` 命名 extract/prepare/queue/render/cleanup [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:151)，Core2d/Core3d 是 camera-driven schedule [schedule.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_core_pipeline/src/schedule.rs:1)。 | `zircon_runtime::graphics::runtime::render_framework::submit_frame_extract`、`scene_renderer::graph_execution`、`render::core_pipeline`。 | 先把同步 submit path 的 extract、context-build、prepare、queue/phase、graph compile、render、postprocess、present、cleanup 阶段全部诊断化；再实现多 camera / 多 target 调度；最后单独设计 pipelined render thread。 | 先跑 scoped core-pipeline/submit tests，再跑 `cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked`；验收必须包含每阶段诊断快照、camera target 覆盖和 culled/executor decision 记录。 | 把当前同步 `submit_frame_extract(...)` 直接称为 Bevy render app parity；只有 RenderStats 聚合计数。 |
| M10.3 Common render API 与资产投影 | Bevy `common_api` 包含 camera/image/mesh/shader/material/text/color/HDR 但不含 renderer [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:46)。 | `render::{camera,image,mesh,shader,material}`、`zircon_runtime::asset::{texture,model,shader,material}`、scene projection。 | 补齐 image/mesh/shader/material descriptor、asset readiness、fallback、依赖诊断；把 `.ztexture` / `.zmesh` / `.zshader` / `.zmaterial` 的 runtime projection 与 render product feature 对齐。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --locked render_product_assets`，再补 importer/asset roundtrip tests；验收文档更新 `render/{image,mesh,shader,material}.md` 与能力矩阵。 | GPU 能画一个 fallback mesh；手写 shader asset 绕过 importer；字符串错误而非结构化 readiness report。 |
| M10.4 Default 2D renderer | Bevy `2d_bevy_render` 建在 `bevy_render`、`bevy_core_pipeline`、`bevy_post_process`、`bevy_sprite_render` 之上 [Cargo.toml](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/Cargo.toml:216)，`SpriteRenderPlugin` 注册 Mesh2d、ColorMaterial、SpriteMesh、Tilemap、extract/queue/bind-group/phase-sort 系统 [bevy_sprite_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_sprite_render/src/lib.rs:63)。 | `render::sprite`、`render::core_pipeline`、`graphics::scene_renderer::sprite`、2D scene components。 | 保持已落地非粒子 sprite path；继续补 Mesh2d / SpriteMesh、atlas/slice/tile mode、batching、per-view pipeline specialization、Text2d 与 picking 只做接口点不吞 UI/picking 里程碑。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --locked render_product_submit sprite`、Core2d pipeline compile tests、atlas/importer roundtrip tests；截图 smoke 只在 presentation 子里程碑通过后作为综合证据。 | 粒子/VG path 或编辑器 gizmo 能显示；只统计 sprite count 不验证材质/图片 fallback。 |
| M10.5 Default 3D / PBR / light | Bevy `3d_bevy_render` 包含 `bevy_anti_alias`、`bevy_gltf`、`bevy_pbr`、`bevy_post_process` [Cargo.toml](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/Cargo.toml:239)；`PbrPlugin` 加载 PBR shader library 并组合 material、SSAO、fog、lightmap、probe、cluster、shadow、transmission、SSR、volumetric fog 等 [bevy_pbr/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_pbr/src/lib.rs:179)。 | `render::{material,light}`、`graphics::scene_renderer::{deferred,post_process,resources}`、scene/editor light authoring。 | 扩展 `StandardMaterialDescriptor` 到 Bevy 常用字段；实现 material bind-group reflection/residency、prepass/deferred/shadow/transparency pipeline specialization；补 point/spot clustered lighting、rect/area light shading、shadow maps、probes/IBL、SSAO/SSR coupling。 | material/light focused unit tests、`render_product_pbr` submit tests、scene asset/editor projection roundtrip、`cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked`；验收必须列出 ready/degraded light/material families。 | 单一 ambient uniform、单个 directional light、forward-only shader 或高级 GI 输出。 |
| M10.6 Post-process 与 anti-alias | Bevy `PostProcessPlugin` 包含 MSAA writeback、bloom、motion blur、DoF、effect stack [bevy_post_process/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_post_process/src/lib.rs:23)；`AntiAliasPlugin` 包含 FXAA、SMAA、TAA、CAS、可选 DLSS [bevy_anti_alias/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_anti_alias/src/lib.rs:21)。 | `render::{post_process,anti_alias}`、`graphics::scene_renderer::post_process`。 | 保持 FXAA fallback 和当前 bloom/color-grading graph；继续补 MSAA writeback/resolve、SMAA LUT、TAA jitter/history、CAS、motion blur、DoF、chromatic aberration、vignette，并把 pass ordering 写成可诊断 graph。 | post-process graph validation tests、AA fallback tests、pipeline/pass ordering tests；验收必须证明缺失 LUT/provider 时退化可见。 | “有 bloom” 或 “有 FXAA” 被当作完整 post-process/AA parity。 |
| M10.7 Presentation / camera target / capture | Bevy camera target 覆盖 window/image/manual texture view/none，screenshot 是异步 target-aware capture [screenshot.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/view/window/screenshot.rs:55)，Core schedule 由 camera target 驱动 [schedule.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_core_pipeline/src/schedule.rs:111)。 | `render::camera`、`submit_frame_extract::build_frame_submission_context`、`viewport_surface`、`graphics::tests::surface_targets`、editor viewport present。 | 把 primary surface、headless/offscreen、texture target、manual/external texture view、no-color/depth-only target 和 screenshot/capture 明确分开；runtime preview 与 editor viewport 走同一 presentation contract。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime camera_target --locked`、surface target tests、capture_frame tests、runtime/editor smoke；验收记录 present-mode fallback、target-size、missing target 和 readback errors。 | PrimarySurface blit 成功；headless fallback 被静默转 primary surface。 |
| M10.8 Render diagnostics / profiling bridge | Bevy `RenderDiagnosticsPlugin` 记录 CPU/GPU pass elapsed time、pipeline statistics，并同步到 `DiagnosticsStore` [diagnostic/mod.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/diagnostic/mod.rs:37)；Bevy profiling 文档要求 tracing spans 和 GPU profiling 分层 [profiling.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/profiling.md:24)。 | `RenderStats`、`zircon_runtime::core::diagnostics::render`、`DiagnosticStore`、dev overlay。 | 把 submit stats 升级为 diagnostic bridge：pass timing、pipeline/cache status、render-asset bytes、mesh allocator bytes、present errors、future render-thread handoff；overlay 只消费 DiagnosticStore。 | render diagnostics snapshot tests、DiagnosticStore log schedule tests、profiling artifact smoke；验收区分 CPU timing、GPU timing、pipeline statistics、resource residency。 | RenderDoc marker、单个 FPS counter、聚合 frame time。 |
| M10.9 AdvancedRender / Solari 分离验收 | Bevy Solari 是实验 plugin group，不属于 default renderer；raytracing scene、realtime Solari、pathtracer 分开。 | `render::advanced`、`render::solari`、`zircon_plugins/{virtual_geometry,hybrid_gi,solari}`。 | AdvancedRender 只在 DefaultRender 通过相应基础门槛后作为增量；Solari 继续报告 capability/provider/gate status，后续再补 BLAS、raytracing scene bindings、ReSTIR/world-cache、DLSS RR、validation pathtracer。 | provider registration tests、capability mismatch tests、Solari unavailable/gated status tests；只作为 advanced acceptance。 | 用 VG/HGI/Solari 结果关闭 M10.2-M10.8 的默认产品缺口。 |

M10 测试阶段命令顺序：先跑 `git diff --check` 与直接 trailing-whitespace scan；再跑 focused tests（`render_profile`、`render_product_assets`、`render_product_submit`、`camera_target`、post-process/AA/core-pipeline/material/light 子集）；再跑 `cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked` 和涉及 app profile 的 `cargo check -p zircon_app --locked --all-targets`；最后进入 `.\.codex\skills\zircon-dev\scripts\validate-matrix.ps1 -Package zircon_runtime` / `-Package zircon_app`，全仓 `validate-matrix.ps1` 留到 asset/render/UI/hub 活跃会话收束后的 M10 milestone testing stage。

#### M10 执行依赖和推进顺序

M10 的实现顺序按“默认产品先闭环，高级产品后证明”的原则推进。Bevy 的 `DefaultPlugins` 顺序先建立 render backend、image、mesh、camera、light，再进入 core pipeline、post-process、AA、sprite/UI/PBR [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:43)；`RenderSystems` 再把执行拆成 extract、prepare assets/meshes/views、queue、phase sort、prepare bind groups、render、cleanup [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:151)。Zircon 不能从 PBR、Solari 或 VG/HGI 倒推基础层完成，必须按下列顺序收口：

| 顺序 | Gate | 必须先完成 | 可并行工作 | 退出门槛 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Profile freeze | M10.1 `DefaultRender` / `Headless` / `AdvancedRender` / `SolariExperimental` profile 语义、模块顺序和非替代规则。 | 文档证据、profile tests、app profile diagnostics。 | `DefaultRender` 的 included profiles、features、capability validation、app config storage 都有测试或文档证据。 |
| 2 | Common API readiness | M10.3 camera/image/mesh/shader/material descriptor 与 asset projection。 | image、mesh、shader、material 的 descriptor docs 可并行；asset importer/runtime projection 需要与 asset lane 协调。 | 所有 default renderer 后续切片能通过结构化 readiness/fallback report 解释缺失资源，而不是靠字符串或 silent fallback。 |
| 3 | Schedule visibility | M10.2 synchronous submit path 的阶段诊断和 graph executor 决策可见化。 | Core2d/Core3d phase queue、submit stats、diagnostics bridge 设计可并行；true render SubApp/pipelined thread 必须后置。 | extract/context-build/prepare/queue/render/postprocess/present/cleanup 至少能在诊断里被单独定位。 |
| 4 | Default 2D and presentation base | M10.4 sprite/Core2d 和 M10.7 primary/headless/capture target 的基础闭环。 | Sprite/Mesh2d 与 presentation target docs 可并行；screenshot smoke 必须等 presentation target error model 清楚后再算综合证据。 | Core2d 不依赖 PBR 或 advanced graph；primary/headless/texture target 行为明确成功、失败或待实现。 |
| 5 | Default 3D / PBR / light | M10.5 material bind groups、PBR pipeline variants、light families、shadows/probes/SSAO/SSR 逐步补齐。 | Material descriptor breadth、light readiness、deferred/forward pipeline diagnostics 可并行；shader/material assetization 要与 asset/material lane 协调。 | 每个 light/material family 有 ready/degraded 证据，PBR 不能只靠 ambient 或 single-directional path 通过。 |
| 6 | Post-process / AA breadth | M10.6 effect families、MSAA writeback、SMAA/TAA/CAS/DLSS capability/provider gates。 | Post-process pass graph validation 和 AA fallback tests 可并行。 | bloom/FXAA 只关闭各自子项，不再代表完整 post-process/AA parity。 |
| 7 | Diagnostics/profiling bridge | M10.8 把 render product stats 接入 `DiagnosticStore` / overlay / profiling artifact。 | CPU timing、GPU timing、pipeline stats、resource bytes 可以分阶段；overlay 必须只消费 diagnostic store。 | 至少能区分 product readiness、pass timing、pipeline/cache state、present/capture failure。 |
| 8 | Advanced/Solari proof | M10.9 在默认 renderer 缺口不被遮蔽的前提下推进 VG/HGI/Solari。 | Provider registration、capability mismatch、Solari unavailable/gated status 可并行。 | Advanced/Solari 只作为增量验收，不能关闭 M10.2-M10.8 的默认产品门槛。 |

#### M10.1 Profile Freeze 验收清单

M10.1 是 M10 的第一个依赖 gate。它只冻结产品词汇、默认 bundle、advanced 分离和 app-level profile storage，不宣称 renderer parity 已完成。Bevy 的压力来自两层：`cargo_features.md` 把 `default`、`2d`、`3d`、`ui`、`common_api` 和 renderer collections 分开 [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:22)，`DefaultPlugins` 先装常规 render/PBR/UI render 切片而不是用实验高级 renderer 代替基础产品 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:43)。

| 检查项 | Bevy 依据 | Zircon 当前证据 | 通过条件 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DefaultRender` 是默认渲染产品 bundle，不是全引擎 default profile。 | `default = 2d + 3d + ui + audio`，renderer collection 另列 [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:22)。 | `RenderProfileBundle::default_render()` 固定为 `CommonRenderApi + Render2d + Render3d + Ui` [profile.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/core/framework/render/profile.rs:78)，测试覆盖基础产品且排除高级 feature [tests.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/core/framework/tests.rs:1050)。 | `cargo test -p zircon_runtime render_profile --locked` 中 default bundle 断言通过。 |
| `DefaultRender` 不携带 VG/HGI/Solari。 | Bevy default render-facing plugin 顺序先覆盖 Image/Mesh/Camera/Light/CorePipeline/PostProcess/AA/Sprite/UI/PBR [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:43)。 | 默认 bundle 不包含 `AdvancedRender`、`SolariExperimental`、`VirtualGeometry`、`HybridGlobalIllumination`、`Solari` [tests.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/core/framework/tests.rs:1050)。 | 任何 M10 验收不得用 VG/HGI/Solari provider 成功替代默认 2D/3D/UI、presentation、diagnostics 证据。 |
| `Headless` 是无渲染产品 profile。 | Bevy `common_api` 明确是 API surface，不包含实际 renderer [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:46)。 | `EntryConfig` 在 headless profile 下选择 `RenderProfileBundle::headless()` [entry_config.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_app/src/entry/entry_config.rs:203)，app test 断言不启用 2D/3D/UI [profile_bootstrap.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_app/src/entry/tests/profile_bootstrap.rs:462)。 | Headless 不能 silent fallback 到 primary surface 或默认 render products。 |
| app bootstrap 在模块依赖前存储 profile。 | Bevy plugin group 配置先于 app execution 生效 [default_plugins.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_internal/src/default_plugins.rs:32)。 | `BuiltinEngineEntry` 把 bundle 存到 `RENDER_PROFILE_CONFIG_KEY`，runtime/headless tests 覆盖 default/headless storage [profile_bootstrap.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_app/src/entry/tests/profile_bootstrap.rs:446)。 | `cargo check -p zircon_app --locked --all-targets` 作为 app-profile promotion check。 |
| Advanced/Solari provider 是显式 opt-in。 | Bevy feature/profile 文档把 optional renderer breadth 显式拆分 [cargo_features.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/docs/cargo_features.md:48)。 | app-side tests 覆盖 default render 不链接高级 provider，advanced/Solari profile 才链接 VG/HGI/Solari provider [profile_bootstrap.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_app/src/entry/tests/profile_bootstrap.rs:219)。 | provider registration tests 只证明 M10.9，不关闭 M10.2-M10.8。 |
| backend capability gate 保持结构化错误。 | Bevy renderer feature 由 renderer/platform 能力约束，不能静默成功。 | profile validation 把 required features、advanced caps、AA caps、Solari caps 映射成 `RenderProfileValidationError` [profile.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/core/framework/render/profile.rs:149)。 | 缺失 capability 必须是结构化 validation error 或 explicit unavailable status。 |

这次 M10.1 清单是 docs-only 增量。只有在新鲜运行 `cargo test -p zircon_runtime render_profile --locked` 和 `cargo check -p zircon_app --locked --all-targets` 后，才能把 profile freeze 从“验收清单已写明”提升为“当前 checkout 已验证通过”。

#### M10.2 Schedule Visibility 验收清单

M10.2 不要求 Zircon 立刻复制 Bevy 的 render sub-app、render world 或 pipelined render thread。它要求当前同步 submit path 的阶段、camera/Core2d/Core3d 选择、graph executor 决策、post-process/present/cleanup 边界可被诊断和验收。Bevy 的依据是 `RenderPlugin` 明确 render 在 `RenderApp` sub-app 中运行 [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:120)，`RenderSystems` 命名 extract/prepare/queue/render/cleanup 等阶段 [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:151)，Core2d/Core3d 是 camera-driven schedules [schedule.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_core_pipeline/src/schedule.rs:31)，`camera_driver` 会遍历 sorted cameras、执行对应 schedule、记录 covered windows [schedule.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_core_pipeline/src/schedule.rs:111)，pipelined rendering 单独引入 render thread 和 `RenderExtractApp` [pipelined_rendering.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/pipelined_rendering.rs:68)。详细文档见 [core_pipeline.md](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/docs/zircon_runtime/core/framework/render/core_pipeline.md)。

| 检查项 | Bevy 依据 | Zircon 当前证据 | 通过条件 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| render ownership 明确。 | `RenderPlugin` 创建 render sub-app、schedule、startup/recovery、pipeline cache 和 render system [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:344)。 | `submit_frame_extract(...)` 是同步 runtime-framework 入口，包含 context-build、prepare、render、feedback、record、release、stats [submit.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/graphics/runtime/render_framework/submit_frame_extract/submit/submit.rs:22)。 | M10.2 可以验收 stage visibility，但不能宣称 Bevy render world/sub-app parity。 |
| stage vocabulary 可观测。 | `RenderSystems` 拆出 ExtractCommands、PrepareAssets、PrepareMeshes、Queue、PhaseSort、Prepare、PrepareBindGroups、Render、Cleanup、PostCleanup [bevy_render/src/lib.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/lib.rs:155)。 | Zircon source 已有 build context、prepare runtime、compile pipeline、execute graph stage、post-process、queue submit、record、update stats。 | 诊断命名必须覆盖 extract/context-build/prepare/queue-or-phase/graph-compile/render/postprocess/present/cleanup。 |
| camera schedule 与 submit 分离。 | Core2d/Core3d 是 per-camera sub-schedules；driver 遍历 cameras 并跟踪 covered windows [schedule.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_core_pipeline/src/schedule.rs:111)。 | `ViewportCameraSnapshot::core_pipeline_kind()` 和 phase queue/pipeline compile tests 覆盖 Core2d/Core3d 选择与阶段顺序。 | true multi-camera、per-target coverage、uncovered surface clear、split-screen、texture target schedule 仍是后续 gate。 |
| graph executor 决策可解释。 | Bevy queue/phase-sort/prepare/render sets 暴露 phase 与 backend transition。 | `compile.rs` 校验 core-pipeline、feature descriptors、extract sections、capability、history bindings、stage mappings、queues、resources [compile.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/graphics/pipeline/render_pipeline_asset/compile.rs:18)；`execute_graph_stage.rs` 记录 executor、stage、queue、dependencies、resources [execute_graph_stage.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/zircon_runtime/src/graphics/scene/scene_renderer/core/scene_renderer_core_render_compiled_scene/render/execute_graph_stage.rs:80)。 | 补齐 culled-pass reason、resource residency、executor availability、effective queue、pass timing 后才能叫 graph scheduling complete。 |
| pipelined rendering 后置。 | Bevy pipelined mode 用 render thread overlap frame N/N+1 [pipelined_rendering.rs](E:/Git/ZirconEngine/dev/bevy/crates/bevy_render/src/pipelined_rendering.rs:68)。 | Zircon 当前同步 submit 只有 profiling scopes/RenderDoc markers，没有 render-thread channel 或 overlap telemetry。 | 不把同步 stats 当作 pipelined proof；后续单独设计 render-thread lifecycle、extract handoff、overlap timing。 |

M10.2 promotion 需要在构建队列空闲时运行 focused core-pipeline/submit tests 与 `cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked`。当前 docs-only 切片只写明 gate 和现有证据，不声明 fresh Cargo 通过。

#### M10.3 Common Render API Readiness 验收清单
